- **deterministic-enforcement**: Decides whether a rule belongs in a prompt or in code, then builds the gate. Use when a tool must never run before a prerequisite, a spend or security limit has to hold every time, or a prompt rule keeps being violated. Covers PreToolUse decisions with actionable reasons, permission rules, and sandboxing. Not for editing settings files (use update-config); not for auditing existing hooks (use review-hooks); not for when to hand off to a human (use provenance-and-escalation). ([CatylAI/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/CatylAI/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/software-development/agent-craft/skills/deterministic-enforcement))

- **deterministic-execution**: Execute known-command tasks in a single deterministic step. Never explore, dry-run, re-read, or summarize when the command and its expected output are fully predictable. Eliminates the anti-pattern of multi-step exploration for trivial operations. ([jeanboutros/tian-lu](https://github.com/jeanboutros/tian-lu/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/deterministic-execution))
- **deterministic-execution-lane**: Runs a resumable, fail-closed implementation lane with explicit checkpoints. Use when a multi-step change must continue until done or blocked, without silent skips. ([SlipstreamAI/ClankerKit](https://github.com/SlipstreamAI/ClankerKit/tree/HEAD/skills/deterministic-execution-lane))

- **deterministic-file-edits**: Recover when the `patch` tool silently fails or reports success without applying — especially on regex literals, backslashes, escaped characters, or generated code. Use a deterministic node `.cjs` script run via `terminal` instead of fuzzy replace. Also covers the isolate-verify-merge workflow for risky/credential-gated features. ([itsPremkumar/Hermes-Full-Autonomous-Company](https://github.com/itsPremkumar/Hermes-Full-Autonomous-Company/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/deterministic-file-edits) · ★4)
- **deterministic-first**: Use before reading files or large output to find, count, extract, parse, verify, loop, or wait. Routes that work to a deterministic shell pipeline so the answer enters context, not the haystack — cheaper and more correct than reading content into the model. ([yoeld-wix/quiet-bash](https://github.com/yoeld-wix/quiet-bash/tree/HEAD/skills/deterministic-first) · ★5)

- **deterministic-metric-design**: Inventing deterministic metrics — turning a fuzzy property like 'maintainability', 'risk', or 'how reducible this code is' into a deterministic, computable number an agent can trust and optimize. Covers the path from construct to adoption — operationalizing the construct, confronting computability limits (Kolmogorov, Rice) with sound proxies, picking the right measurement scale, proving properties (monotonicity, invariance, the Weyuker/Briand axioms), guaranteeing determinism, establishing construct validity (not just LOC in disguise), and hardening against Goodhart-style gaming when an agent… ([pproenca/dot-skills](https://github.com/pproenca/dot-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/.curated/deterministic-metric-design) · ★228)
